# One Piece per-print TCGplayer review queue

Research-only, 608 distinct prints in 262 collector-number families. This table preserves one row per canonical artwork/treatment; no siblings are merged. The links on print IDs point to official reference images. Product links are observed TCGCSV catalog URLs, not invented IDs.

Source: [full evidence](one-piece-tcgplayer-alignment-2026-09-07.json), feed as of 2026-09-06T20:05:30+0000, fetched 2026-09-07T17:01:16.462Z. Historical image comparisons are dated in each JSON record. Fresh product-image comparison was unavailable because the CDN robots request returned HTTP 403. Historical candidates are not freshly verified or human-approved. Prices are USD aggregate references, not checkout totals or ranked listings.

[Findings and direct-page checks](one-piece-tcgplayer-alignment-2026-09-07.md). For rows without historical mappings, all retained same-number candidates are shown; shared names/numbers do not prove an exact match. The JSON contains each retained candidate's price subtypes, timestamps, and conflicts.
